gaokao_000_001 ✓ A A 298 https://api.deepseek.com
Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

2. 已知平面向量\vec{a}，\vec{b}不共线，且2\vec{a} + y\vec{b} = x\vec{a} - 3\vec{b}，则（ ）
A. x=2, y=-3
B. x=-2, y=3
C. x=2, y=3
D. x=-2, y=-3

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
因为 \(\vec a,\vec b\) 不共线，所以它们线性无关。

由  
\[
2\vec a+y\vec b=x\vec a-3\vec b
\]
得  
\[
(2-x)\vec a+(y+3)\vec b=0
\]

由于 \(\vec a,\vec b\) 不共线，所以  
\[
2-x=0,\quad y+3=0
\]
因此  
\[
x=2,\quad y=-3
\]

答案：\(\boxed{A}\)
Reasoning
We need answer in Chinese presumably. Need solve. Need be careful: problem statement: "已知平面向量 a, b 不共线，且 2a + y b = x a - 3 b，则（ ）" We need infer question asks values? Since a,b not collinear, coefficients of a and b must match: 2 = x, y = -3. So A. Need put final answer in boxed. We can provide reasoning concise. Need include \boxed{A}. But let's consider if any trick: equation 2\vec a + y\vec b = x\vec a -3\vec b. Rearranged (2-x)\vec a + (y+3)\vec b =0. Since a,b not collinear, linearly independent, coefficients zero. So x=2,y=-3. Option A. Final with box.

\[
y=13x-8
\]

所以答案是：

\boxed{D.\ y=13x-8}
\]

A. \frac{1}{2}
B. 1
C. \frac{3}{2}
D. 2

Response

\[
x_0+2=e^{x_0}+a. \tag{1}
\]

又

\[
f'(x)=\frac{a-(x+1)e^x}{(e^x+a)^2},
\]

\[
a=(x_0+1)e^{x_0}. \tag{2}
\]

将 (2) 代入 (1)：

\[
x_0+2=e^{x_0}+(x_0+1)e^{x_0}=(x_0+2)e^{x_0},
\]

所以

\[
(x_0+2)(1-e^{x_0})=0.
\]

\(x_0=-2\) 不可能，因此 \(e^{x_0}=1\)，得 \(x_0=0\)。

代入 (2)：

\[
a=(0+1)e^0=1.
\]

检验：当 \(a=1\) 时，\(f(0)=1\)，且 \(f(x)\le 1\)，符合最大值为 1。

\[
\boxed{\text{B（}a=1\text{）}}
\]

\[
a_1,a_2,\ldots,a_{12}=1,3,3,5,5,7,9,11,13,15,17,19

\[

设6个两数之和构成等差数列：

\[
t,\ t+d,\ t+2d,\ldots,t+5d
\]

则

\[
6t+15d=108
\]

即

\[
2t+5d=36
\]

又因为所有 \(a_i\) 都是奇数，所以每组两个奇数之和为偶数。

检验选项：

- \(d=2\)，则 \(t=13\)，全为奇数，不可能；

  \[

  \[
  8,12,16,20,24,28

  公差为 \(4\)；
- \(d=6\)，则 \(t=3\)，全为奇数，不可能；

\boxed{\text{B }(4)}

8. 设U={(x_1, x_2, x_3)|x_i \in \{-2,-1,1,2"}, i=1,2,3}\}为空间中64个点构成的集合，点P(1,1,1)，记样本空间\Omega = C_U P，从\Omega中随机取一个点，定义随机变量X如下：对\Omega中的每个点A(x_1, x_2, x_3)，令X(A) = x_1 + x_2 + x_3，则X的数学期望值为（ ）
A. -\frac{1}{21}
B. -\frac{1}{63}
C. 0
D. \frac{1}{7}

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
对每一坐标，取值集合 \(\{-2,-1,1,2\}\) 的和为  
\[
-2-1+1+2=0.
\]

\[

\]

\[
X(P)=1+1+1=3.
\]
所以
\[
\sum_{A\in\Omega}X(A)=0-3=-3,\qquad |\Omega|=63.
\]
因此
\[
E[X]=\frac{-3}{63}=-\frac{1}{21}.
\]

Response

逐项判断：

- A. \(\bar z=3-2i\)，正确。
- B. \(|z|=\sqrt{3^2+2^2}=\sqrt{13}\neq 5\)，错误。

- D.  
  \[
  \frac{z+3}{z-i}=\frac{6+2i}{3+i}
  =\frac{(6+2i)(3-i)}{(3+i)(3-i)}
  =\frac{20}{10}=2\in \mathbf{R}
  \]
  正确。

所以答案为：

\[
\boxed{\text{A、C、D}}
\]

gaokao_000_009 ✓ BC BC 11406 https://api.deepseek.com
Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

10. 在空间中，A、B为两个定点，动点C到直线AB的距离为2，动点D到直线AB的距离为1。若二面角C - AB - D为60°，则（ ）
A. \angle CAD \ge 60^\circ
B. CD \ge \sqrt{3}
C. 当AB \perp CD时，CD \perp平面ABD
D. 当AB \perp平面ACD时，AC \perp AD

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
设直线 \(AB\) 为 \(x\) 轴，\(A\) 为原点。  
令  
\[
C=(x_1,2\cos\alpha,2\sin\alpha),\quad D=(x_2,\cos\beta,\sin\beta)
\]
因为二面角 \(C-AB-D=60^\circ\)，所以两径向向量夹角为 \(60^\circ\)，故
\[
(2\cos\alpha,2\sin\alpha)\cdot(\cos\beta,\sin\beta)=2\cos(\alpha-\beta)=1.
\]

逐项判断：

- A 不一定成立：例如取 \(x_1=x_2=2\) 时，\(\angle CAD<60^\circ\)，故 A 错。
- B：  
\[
CD^2=(x_2-x_1)^2+4+1-2\cdot 1=(x_2-x_1)^2+3\ge 3
\]
所以 \(CD\ge \sqrt3\)，B 正确。
- C：若 \(AB\perp CD\)，则 \(x_1=x_2\)。此时
\[
\overrightarrow{CD}\cdot \overrightarrow{AD}
=(D-C)\cdot D
=|D|^2-C\cdot D
=(x_1^2+1)-(x_1^2+1)=0
\]
且 \(CD\perp AB\)，所以 \(CD\perp\) 平面 \(ABD\)，C 正确。
- D：若 \(AB\perp\) 平面 \(ACD\)，则 \(x_1=x_2=0\)，于是
\[
\overrightarrow{AC}\cdot \overrightarrow{AD}=1\ne 0
\]
故 \(AC\not\perp AD\)，D 错。

因此答案为  
\[
\boxed{BC}
\]
Reasoning
We need answer in Chinese. Need solve problem. Need be careful. We need analyze geometry.

Problem statement: In space, A,B fixed points. Moving point C has distance to line AB = 2, moving point D has distance to line AB = 1. The dihedral angle C-AB-D is 60°. Which statements true?
Options:
A. angle CAD >= 60°
B. CD >= sqrt(3)
C. When AB ⟂ CD, then CD ⟂ plane ABD
D. When AB ⟂ plane ACD, then AC ⟂ AD

Need determine correct? Maybe single/multiple? It's a multiple choice? Need answer options.

Let's set coordinate system.

Let line AB be z-axis or x-axis. Take A,B fixed on x-axis, say line AB = x-axis. Any point C with distance to line AB =2 means its perpendicular distance to x-axis =2. Represent C = (x_C, r_C cos θ_C, r_C sin θ_C) with r_C =2. D distance to x-axis =1: D=(x_D, cos θ_D, sin θ_D) radius 1. The dihedral angle C-AB-D is angle between half-planes determined by line AB and points C,D; since radii perpendicular to axis, the dihedral angle equals angle between radial vectors from foot to C and foot to D in plane perpendicular to AB: θ = |θ_C - θ_D| modulo maybe 60° (or 120? dihedral usually smaller angle between planes =60, so radial angle acute? Actually angle between planes = angle between normal vectors to planes? Let's derive).

Plane C-AB is span x-axis and u. Plane D-AB span x-axis and v. The dihedral angle along AB equals angle between u and v? Since both planes contain x-axis, angle between planes equals angle between their normal vectors? Actually angle between planes is angle between normal vectors or supplement; within half-planes containing C,D, the dihedral angle is the angle between rays from AB to C and D in the cross-section perpendicular to AB. So yes angle between u and v = 60° (assuming smaller angle). Thus dot product u·v = |u||v| cos60 =2*1*1/2=1. So (2cosα)(cosβ)+(2sinα)(sinβ)=2 cos(α-β)=1 => cos(α-β)=1/2 => |α-β|=60° or 300 etc. Good.

Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

11. 已知圆C_1:(x+1)^2+y^2=1，圆C_2:(x-1)^2+y^2=1，圆C_3:x^2+(y-\sqrt{3})^2=1，直线l:y=kx+b与C_1, C_2, C_3均有两个交点。记l被C_1, C_2, C_3截得的弦长分别为s_1、s_2、s_3，则（ ）
A. k可以取任意实数
B. 满足s_1=s_2=s_3的直线l共有3条
C. 满足s_1+s_2+s_3=3的直线l多于3条
D. 当b=0时，s_1+s_2+s_3的最大值为\frac{2\sqrt{21}}{3}

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
设 \(t=\sqrt{k^2+1}\)。直线可写为 \(kx-y+b=0\)，所以三圆心到直线的距离分别为  
\[
d_1=\frac{|b-k|}{t},\quad d_2=\frac{|b+k|}{t},\quad d_3=\frac{|b-\sqrt3|}{t}.
\]
与圆有两个交点需 \(d_i<1\)，且弦长
\[
s_i=2\sqrt{1-d_i^2}.
\]

**A 错。**  
取 \(k=\frac1{\sqrt3}\)，则 \(t=\frac2{\sqrt3}\)。前两个条件给出
\[
b\in\left(-\frac1{\sqrt3},\frac1{\sqrt3}\right),
\]
第三个条件给出
\[
b\in\left(\frac1{\sqrt3},\frac5{\sqrt3}\right),
\]
交集为空，故 \(k\) 不能取任意实数。

**B 对。**  
\(s_1=s_2=s_3\iff d_1=d_2=d_3\)，即
\[
|b-k|=|b+k|=|b-\sqrt3|.
\]
由 \(|b-k|=|b+k|\) 得 \(bk=0\)。

- 若 \(k=0\)，则 \(|b|=|b-\sqrt3|\)，得 \(b=\frac{\sqrt3}{2}\)；
- 若 \(b=0\)，则 \(|k|=\sqrt3\)，得 \(k=\pm\sqrt3\)。

共 3 条直线，且都满足 \(d_i=\frac{\sqrt3}{2}<1\)，所以 B 正确。

**C 对。**  
B 中三条直线都有 \(s_i=1\)，所以都满足 \(s_1+s_2+s_3=3\)。  
再取水平线 \(k=0\)，令 \(y=b\)，则
\[
F(b)=4\sqrt{1-b^2}+2\sqrt{1-(b-\sqrt3)^2}.
\]
计算得
\[
F(0.74)\approx 2.942<3,\qquad F(0.75)\approx 3.023>3.
\]
由连续性，存在 \(b_0\in(0.74,0.75)\) 使 \(F(b_0)=3\)，得到另一条满足条件的直线。因此满足条件的直线多于 3 条，C 正确。

**D 对。**  
当 \(b=0\) 时，需 \(k^2>2\)。
\[
s_1=s_2=\frac{2}{\sqrt{k^2+1}},\qquad
s_3=\frac{2\sqrt{k^2-2}}{\sqrt{k^2+1}}.
\]
令 \(q=\sqrt{k^2-2}>0\)，则
\[
S=s_1+s_2+s_3
=2\cdot \frac{q+2}{\sqrt{q^2+3}}.
\]
求导得最大值在 \(q=\frac32\) 处取得：
\[
S_{\max}=2\cdot \frac{\frac72}{\sqrt{\frac{21}{4}}}
=\frac{2\sqrt{21}}{3}.
\]
所以 D 正确。

因此答案为  
\[
\boxed{BCD}
\]
Reasoning

Let's plan final:

1. Set line standard \(kx-y+b=0\), \(m=\sqrt{k^2+1}\).
2. Distances:
\[
d_1=\frac{|b-k|}{m},\quad d_2=\frac{|b+k|}{m},\quad d_3=\frac{|b-\sqrt3|}{m}.
\]
Need \(d_i<1\). Chord \(s_i=2\sqrt{1-d_i^2}\).

3. A:
For \(k=1/\sqrt3\), \(m=2/\sqrt3\).
Conditions for b:
\[
b\in\left(-\frac1{\sqrt3},\frac1{\sqrt3}\right) \cap \left(\frac1{\sqrt3},\frac{5}{\sqrt3}\right)=\varnothing.
\]
So no such line; A false. Need perhaps explain where intersection comes from:
From d1<1 => |b-k|<m; d2 => |b+k|<m; intersection gives (-1/√3,1/√3); d3 => |b-√3|<m gives (1/√3,5/√3). Empty. Good.

4. B:
Equal chord lengths => equal distances. \(|b-k|=|b+k|\) => \(bk=0\).
- \(k=0\): \(|b|=|b-\sqrt3|\) => \(b=\sqrt3/2\).
- \(b=0\): \(|k|=\sqrt3\) => \(k=\pm\sqrt3\).
These 3 all meet d<1. So B true.

5. C:
From B, three lines ( \(y=\sqrt3/2\), \(y=\pm\sqrt3 x\)) satisfy sum=3. It remains to show another line:
Take \(k=0\), \(l:y=b\), \(b\in(\sqrt3-1,1)\). Then
\[
F(b)=4\sqrt{1-b^2}+2\sqrt{1-(b-\sqrt3)^2}.
\]
Compute \(F(0.74)\approx2.942<3\), \(F(0.75)\approx3.023>3\). Therefore exists \(b_0\in(0.74,0.75)\) with \(F(b_0)=3\). This line is distinct from the above three. Hence at least 4 lines; C true.

6. D:
Set \(b=0\). Conditions: \(|k|<m\) automatic, \(|-\sqrt3|<m\) => \(k^2>2\). Let \(m=\sqrt{k^2+1}\).
\[
s_1=s_2=\frac{2}{m},\quad s_3=\frac{2\sqrt{k^2-2}}{m}.
\]
Thus
\[
S=\frac{4+2\sqrt{k^2-2}}{\sqrt{k^2+1}}.
\]
Let \(q=\sqrt{k^2-2}>0\). Then
\[
S=2\frac{q+2}{\sqrt{q^2+3}}.
\]
Derivative:
\[
\frac{d}{dq}\left(\frac{q+2}{\sqrt{q^2+3}}\right)=\frac{3-2q}{(q^2+3)^{3/2}}.
\]
Maximum at \(q=3/2\), giving
\[
S_{\max}=2\cdot \frac{7/2}{\sqrt{9/4+3}} = \frac{7}{\sqrt{21}/2}=\frac{2\sqrt{21}}{3}.
\]
D true.

请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

12. 双曲线5x^2 - 6y^2 = 1的离心率为__________.

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
双曲线化为标准形式：
\[
5x^2-6y^2=1 \Rightarrow \frac{x^2}{\frac15}-\frac{y^2}{\frac16}=1
\]
所以
\[
a^2=\frac15,\quad b^2=\frac16
\]
离心率
\[
e=\frac{c}{a}=\sqrt{\frac{a^2+b^2}{a^2}}
=\sqrt{\frac{\frac15+\frac16}{\frac15}}
=\sqrt{\frac{11}{6}}
=\frac{\sqrt{66}}{6}
\]

\[
\boxed{\frac{\sqrt{66}}{6}}
\]
Reasoning
We need answer in Chinese probably. Need solve. Need compute eccentricity of hyperbola 5x^2 - 6y^2 =1. Need standard form: x^2/(1/5) - y^2/(1/6)=1. So a^2=1/5, b^2=1/6. For hyperbola, c^2 = a^2 + b^2 = 1/5 + 1/6 = 11/30. Eccentricity e = c/a = sqrt((a^2+b^2)/a^2)= sqrt(c^2/a^2)= sqrt((11/30)/(1/5)) = sqrt(11/30 * 5)= sqrt(11/6)= sqrt(66)/6? Since sqrt(11/6)=√66/6. Alternatively e=√(1 + b^2/a^2)=√(1+(1/6)/(1/5))=√(1+5/6)=√(11/6). Final in box. Need maybe just answer. Ensure \boxed{\dfrac{\sqrt{66}}{6}} or \sqrt{11/6}. Which is expected? e = c/a = sqrt(11/30)/sqrt(1/5)=sqrt( (11/30)*5)=sqrt(11/6). Rationalized: sqrt(11/6)=√66/6. Both. Usually fill blank maybe \frac{\sqrt{66}}{6}. Let's craft concise with steps.

gaokao_000_012 ✗ \frac{3\pi}{2}, 1 \theta=\frac{3\pi}{2},\quad f\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)=1 10461 https://api.deepseek.com
Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

13. 已知f(x)=2sin(ax+\theta) (a \in Z, 0 \le \theta < 2\pi)是偶函数，f(x)在区间(0, \frac{\pi}{2})单调递增。则\theta =__________，f(\frac{2\pi}{3}) =__________.

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
由 \(f\) 为偶函数：

\[
f(-x)=f(x)\Rightarrow \sin(ax+\theta)=\sin(\theta-ax)
\]

所以

\[
\sin(ax+\theta)-\sin(\theta-ax)=2\cos\theta\sin(ax)=0
\]

对任意 \(x\) 成立。又因为 \(f\) 在 \((0,\frac{\pi}{2})\) 上严格递增，故不是常函数，所以 \(a\ne 0\)，从而

\[
\cos\theta=0
\]

在 \(0\le \theta<2\pi\) 中，得

\[
\theta=\frac{\pi}{2}\text{ 或 }\frac{3\pi}{2}
\]

若 \(\theta=\frac{\pi}{2}\)，则

\[
f(x)=2\cos(ax)=2\cos(|a|x)
\]

在 \(0\) 附近递减，不合题意。故

\[
\theta=\frac{3\pi}{2}
\]

此时

\[
f(x)=2\sin\left(ax+\frac{3\pi}{2}\right)=-2\cos(ax)
\]

令 \(b=|a|\)，要使 \(f(x)=-2\cos(bx)\) 在 \((0,\frac{\pi}{2})\) 上递增，需 \(b=1\) 或 \(2\)。因此

\[
f\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)=-2\cos\left(a\cdot \frac{2\pi}{3}\right)=1
\]

\[
\boxed{\theta=\frac{3\pi}{2},\quad f\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)=1}
\]
Reasoning
We need answer in Chinese likely. Need solve problem. Need be careful. Need understand problem.

We have f(x)=2 sin(a x + theta), a ∈ Z, 0 ≤ theta < 2π, is even function, f(x) increasing on interval (0, π/2). Find theta = ____, f(2π/3)= ____.

Need solve with reasoning.

Let's formulate final elegantly:

```
由偶函数：
sin(ax+θ)=sin(θ-ax)
=> sin(ax+θ)-sin(θ-ax)=2cosθ sin(ax)=0.
因 f 在 (0,π/2) 上严格递增，不是常函数，故 a≠0，所以 cosθ=0。
于是 θ=π/2 或 3π/2。

若 θ=π/2，则 f=2cos ax=2cos(|a|x)，在 0 附近递减，矛盾；
所以 θ=3π/2，f=-2cos ax。

设 b=|a|，则 f=-2cos bx。要使其在 (0,π/2) 递增，需 f'=2b sin bx>0 于 (0,π/2)，故 bπ/2≤π，即 b≤2，所以 b=1 或 2。

因此 a·(2π/3)=±2π/3 或 ±4π/3，其余弦值均为 -1/2。
f(2π/3)= -2cos(a·2π/3)=1。
```

Need maybe derivative sign "需 f'=2b sin bx>0" if b=2, f'=4 sin2x >0 for 0<x<π/2. If b=1, >0. If b=0? b=|a|>0. Good.

gaokao_000_013 ✗ \sqrt[3]{\frac{3}{2}} \sqrt[3]{\frac32} 9730 https://api.deepseek.com
Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

14. 设实数q满足：存在数列{a_n}，使得对于任意n \in N^*，均有a_1 + a_2 + \cdots + a_{3n} = n^2 + n，且{a_n}中有某连续9项a_k, a_{k+1}, \cdots, a_{k+8}是公比为q的等比数列。则q的最大值为__________.

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
设第 \(j\) 个三项块的和为  
\[
B_j=a_{3j-2}+a_{3j-1}+a_{3j}.
\]
由
\[
a_1+\cdots+a_{3n}=n^2+n
\]
可得
\[
B_j=(j^2+j)-((j-1)^2+(j-1))=2j.
\]
所以每个连续三项块的和必须是 \(2,4,6,\dots\)。

若某连续 9 项构成公比为 \(q\) 的等比数列，设首项为 \(x\)，即
\[
a_k,a_{k+1},\dots,a_{k+8}=x,xq,\dots,xq^8.
\]

分三种情况：

### 1. \(k\equiv 1\pmod 3\)
此时 9 项正好对应三个完整三项块，有
\[
x(1+q+q^2)=2m,\quad xq^3(1+q+q^2)=2(m+1),\quad xq^6(1+q+q^2)=2(m+2).
\]
相除得
\[
q^3=\frac{m+1}{m}=\frac{m+2}{m+1},
\]
不可能。

### 2. \(k\equiv 2\pmod 3\) 或 \(k\equiv 0\pmod 3\)
此时 9 项中包含两个完整三项块，可推出
\[
q^3=\frac{m+2}{m+1}\qquad (m\ge 1).
\]
因此
\[
q^3\le \frac{3}{2},
\]
所以
\[
q\le \sqrt[3]{\frac32}.
\]

当 \(q=\sqrt[3]{\frac32}\) 时，可取适当的首项使得第 \(2\) 到第 \(10\) 项成公比为 \(q\) 的等比数列，并令其它项补齐各三项块的和为 \(2n\)，因此该上界可以达到。

所以最大值为
\[
\boxed{\sqrt[3]{\frac32}}.
\]
Reasoning
We need answer problem in Chinese. Need solve. Let's restate.

Problem: Let real q satisfy: there exists sequence {a_n} such that for every n in N^* (positive integers), a_1 + ... + a_{3n} = n^2 + n, and among {a_n} there exist some consecutive 9 terms a_k,...,a_{k+8} forming a geometric progression with common ratio q. Find maximum q.

Need solve.

We need understand constraints. Given partial sums at multiples of 3: S_{3n} = n^2 + n for all n>=1. This implies conditions on sums of blocks of length 3.

Let b_n = a_{3n-2}+a_{3n-1}+a_{3n}, sum of nth block of 3 terms. Then S_{3n}=∑_{i=1}^n b_i = n^2+n. Thus b_n = (n^2+n)-((n-1)^2+(n-1)) = 2n for n>=1. So for each n, a_{3n-2}+a_{3n-1}+a_{3n}=2n. There are no constraints on partial sums outside multiples of 3? The condition only for all n, so yes only block sums equal 2n. Thus sequence arbitrary real numbers with each consecutive triple sum equal 2,4,6,... corresponding n.

Need existence of some 9 consecutive terms forming geometric progression with ratio q. Since only block sums constrained, we can choose sequence to allow q. Want maximum real q possible.

x q^5(1+q+q^2)=2(m+2).
Divide: q^3=(m+2)/(m+1). Therefore q^3 ∈ {(m+2)/(m+1): m≥1} ⊆ (1,3/2]; hence q≤(3/2)^{1/3}.
- If k≡0 mod3, say k=3m, complete blocks B_{m+1},B_{m+2}: B_{m+1}=x q(1+q+q^2)=2(m+1), B_{m+2}=x q^4(1+q+q^2)=2(m+2). Divide: q^3=(m+2)/(m+1), same bound.
